# Summer 2027 Software Engineering Internships: All Roles

Read from employers' public job boards (Greenhouse, Workday, Lever, Ashby, SmartRecruiters and more) and refreshed every 30 minutes by [swejoblists.com](https://swejoblists.com/?type=internship&utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme&utm_campaign=internships) (free, no account). Last update: 2026-11-21 20:25 UTC.

This file lists every open role, whatever its H-1B label. The [README](README.md) shows only the roles labelled H-1B 🟢 Yes or 🟡 Likely.

Looking for new grad roles? See [TheoXiong7/2027-New-Grad-SWE-Jobs](https://github.com/TheoXiong7/2027-New-Grad-SWE-Jobs).

**3,272 open roles** from **898 companies**, **353** added in the last 7 days. Filters, company pages and match scores: [swejoblists.com/?type=internship](https://swejoblists.com/?type=internship&utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme&utm_campaign=internships).

**H-1B column**: 🟢 Yes / 🟡 Likely / 🟠 Unlikely / 🔴 No = how likely the employer sponsors an H-1B visa for the role; — = not labelled yet. A model reads each posting; it is a screen, not a guarantee.
